    主席報告

    I am pleased to present the annual results of Da Yu Financial Holdings Limited (the      本人欣然發表大禹金融控股有限公司（「本公司」）及其附屬公司
    “Company”) and its subsidiaries (collectively the “Group”) for the year ended 31         （統稱「本集團」）截至二零二五年十二月三十一日止年度之年度
    December 2025.                                                                           業績。


    In 2025, the Hang Seng Index staged a definitive 28% rally, marking its most robust      二零二五年，恒生指數強勢上漲28%，創下自二零一七年以來
    annual performance since 2017. This resurgence was anchored by a dominant “de-           最強勁的年度表現。這一復甦的核心是受「去美元化」主導敘
    dollarization” narrative; as a softening greenback prompted global investors to rotate   事所推動；儘管地緣政治局勢（尤其是中美之間）持續緊張，但
    capital into undervalued ex-US assets, in spite of the ongoing geopolitical saga,        美元走軟促使全球投資者將資金轉向被低估的非美國資產。這
    especially between the United States and China. This “valuation trough” recovery         種「估值觸底」的復甦進一步受到「人工智能交易」
                                                                                                                   （ 由國內大
    was further propelled by the “AI trade” — ignited by domestic breakthroughs in large     型語言模型的突破性進展引發）以及創紀錄的南向資金流入的
    language models — and record-breaking southbound capital inflows, as mainland            推動，伴隨內地家庭從房地產板塊轉向具流動性更高的香港股
    households pivoted from the property sector toward the liquidity of Hong Kong’s          市。受惠於市場的蓬勃氣氛，本集團旗下資產管理業務的基金
    equity market. The Group benefited from a buoyant market through satisfactory            表現令人滿意。
    performance of funds under its asset management activities.

Goodwill Assessment and Impairment                                                     商譽評估及減值

An accounting goodwill of approximately HK$303.0 million was recognised when           根據香港財務報告準則第3號（經修訂）
                                                                                                        「業務合併」，本集團於
the Group acquired Yu Ming in 2019, based on Hong Kong Financial Reporting             二零一九年收購禹銘時確認了約303.0百萬港元之會計商譽。
Standard 3 (Revised) Business Combinations. A goodwill is continuously subject to      商譽須持續進行減值測試，並至少每年一次。
impairment testing and at least annually.


Impairment testing involves assessing the value in use of the cash-generating          減值測試包括評估商譽所分配之現金產生單位、禹銘的「企業
units, the “corporate finance services, investment and others unit” and the “asset     融資服務、投資及其他單位」以及「資產管理服務單位」之使用
management services unit” of Yu Ming in the case of the Group, to which the            價值。此項評估需要本公司估計該等單位之預期未來現金流
goodwill is allocated. This assessment requires the Company to estimate the            量，並使用適當的折現率（不時轉變）以釐定該等現金流量之現
expected future cash flows from these units and to use an appropriate discount rate    值。
(which varies from time to time) to determine the present value of those cash flows.


During the estimation of expected future cash flows, management must consider          於估計預期未來現金流量時，管理層必須考慮有關未來收益及
assumptions regarding future revenue and profits, which are contingent upon future     溢利之假設，該等假設取決於未來事件及狀況。此外，折現率
events and circumstances. Additionally, the discount rate depends on, inter alia,      亦取決於（其中包括）市場利率、市場風險及資產特定風險因
market interest rates, market risk and asset-specific risk factors.                    素。


The board of Directors of the Company (the “Board”) carefully assessed the             本公司董事會（「董事會」）通過對當前的經濟環境及市場情況
necessity for goodwill impairment by conducting a thorough analysis of the current     進行全面分析，仔細評估了商譽減值的必要性，並最終考慮於
economic climate and market conditions, and finally considered to recognise a          二零二五年確認商譽減值約16.2百萬港元（二零二四年：無）。
goodwill impairment of approximately HK$16.2 million in 2025 (2024: Nil). After        撇除非現金性商譽減值的影響，本公司截至二零二五年十二月
excluding the effect of non-cash goodwill impairment, the consolidated financial       三十一日止十二個月的綜合財務業績顯示，本報告期間錄得經
results of the Company for the 12 months ended 31 December 2025 recorded               調整全面收益總額約56.2百萬港元，而去年商譽減值前經調整
an adjusted total comprehensive income for the Current Reporting Period of             全面收益總額為約21.3百萬港元。
approximately HK$56.2 million, compared to the adjusted total comprehensive
income before goodwill impairment of approximately HK$21.3 million in the previous
year.

     Credit approval processes                                                                       信貸審批程序


     Before a loan is granted, the head of lending department (“LDH”) with the assistance            在授予貸款之前，貸款部主管（「貸款部主管」）在外判代理之協
     from outsourcing agents, has to obtain and check on the background information of               助下，必須在初始階段獲取並檢查潛在借款人╱擔保人（如適
     the potential borrower/guarantor (if applicable) including identity document, income            用）之背景資料，除其他事項外包括身份證明文件、收入證明
     proof and residential proof, among other things at the initial stage. Upon receiving            及住址證明等。收到貸款申請表後，貸款部將首先進行了解客
     the loan application forms, KYC procedures will firstly be carried out and credit risk          戶程序，然後進行信貸風險評估。在授予貸款前，將考慮潛在
     assessment will then be conducted by the lending department. Background of the                  客戶╱企業客戶之背景（也將考慮其實益擁有人之背景）、貸款
     potential client/corporate client (the background of its beneficial owners will also be         目的、還款資金來源、抵押品或擔保之價值（如適用）以及所有
     considered), purpose of the loan, source of fund for repayment, value of collateral             被認為必要之資訊。重續貸款前，貸款部主管將獲得最新的盡
     or guarantee (if applicable) and all information which is regarded necessary will be            職調查資訊，並再次進行信貸風險評估。
     considered before granting of the loan. Before renewal of loans, LDH would obtain
     up-to-date due diligence information and perform credit risk assessment again.


     All loans must have a good and reasonable repayment prospect and loan recovery                  所有貸款必須具有良好且合理之還款前景，並且嚴格落實貸款
     actions will be strictly undertaken to ensure the repayment of the loans. LDH is also           追收行動，以確保償還貸款。貸款部主管亦負責確保日常根據
     responsible for ensuring on a daily basis that the relevant operational procedures              本集團貸款政策（「貸款政策」）及物業貸款操作程序手冊而實施
     and controls implemented are adequate and effective in accordance with the                      之相關營運程序及監控措施乃足夠及有效。
     Group’s Money Lending Policy (the “MLP”) and the Property Loan Operation
     Procedures Manual.

Credits are granted according to the hierarchy of approval authorities within the       信貸乃根據本集團內之審批機關層級按董事會訂定之管制額度
Group, based on a control limit determined by the Board, including the LDH and the      授出，包括貸款部主管及本集團內各公司之董事會以及本公司
boards of directors of the respective companies within the Group and the Board, if      董事會，如適用。
applicable.


This control limit, also specified in the MLP, is set according to the materiality of   該管制額度（也於貸款政策中明確說明）乃根據董事會不時決定
the loan exposure decided by the Board from time to time. Any single loan amount        之貸款風險之重要性水平而設定。任何一筆貸款金額超過管制
exceeding the control limit has to be approved by the Board.                            額度均須經董事會批准。


Impairment provisions of mortgage loans and term loans                                  按揭貸款及有期貸款之減值撥備


Assessments are done based on the Group’s historical credit loss experience,            評估乃根據本集團過往信貸虧損經驗而進行，並按債務人之特
adjusted for factors that are specific to the debtors, general economic conditions      定因素、一般經濟環境以及評估報告當日之目前情況及未來情
and an assessment of both the current conditions at the reporting date as well as the   況之預測而作出調整。
forecast of future conditions.


The expected credit loss (“ECL”) for term loans is assessed individually and            有期貸款之預期信貸虧損（「預期信貸虧損」）按個別評估，而按
mortgage loans is assessed collectively. In determining impairment allowances on        揭貸款之預期信貸虧損按集體評估。在確定有期貸款及按揭貸
term loans and mortgage loans, the measurement of ECL requires estimation of the        款之減值撥備時，預期信貸虧損之計量需要估計及預期未來現
amounts in respect of expected future cash flows and the assessment of whether          金流量之金額，並評估信貸風險是否有顯著增加。
there have been a significant increase in credit risk.


The impairment provision has taken into account the financial strength of the           減值撥備已考慮借款人之財務實力以及抵押品及其他信貸增強
borrowers as well as the quality of collateral and other credit enhancements.           措施之質量。


In respect of secured mortgage loans with the carrying amount of approximately          有關於二零二五年十二月三十一日之有抵押按揭貸款賬面值約
HK$6.7 million as at 31 December 2025 (2024: approximately HK$8.3 million), the         6.7百萬港元（二零二四年：約8.3百萬港元），而該等按揭貸款
realisable value of collateral of such mortgage loans can be objectively ascertained    之抵押品之可實現價值，根據於財政年度結束時進行之評估，
to cover the outstanding loan amounts based on valuations conducted at the end of       是可客觀確定為足夠償付未償還之貸款金額。
the financial year.


The loan to value ratio, calculated by dividing the outstanding loans of each           於二零二五年十二月三十一日，貸款與估值比率（乃將每筆
mortgage loan by the appraised value of its property pledged, ranged from 1.5% to       按揭貸款之未償還貸款金額除以其抵押物業之估值計算）介乎
57.9% as at 31 December 2025 (2024: 0.5% to 58.4%).                                     1.5%至57.9%（二零二四年：0.5%至58.4%）。

     PRINCIPAL RISKS AND UNCERTAINTIES                                                       主要風險及不確定因素

     Due to the nature of our corporate finance advisory services of non-repetitive basis,   由於我們企業融資顧問服務具有非重複性特點，因此，企業融
     income from corporate finance business is volatile, and usually decreases when          資業務收入波動較大，並且在年內（如二零二五年）較少特別交

PROSPECT                                                                               前景

Our corporate finance business is mostly driven by the transactions mandates           我們的企業融資業務主要由年內獲得的交易委託書推動，我們
secured during the year and our team will remain active in specialty works which       的團隊將繼續積極從事我們以能力著稱的專業工作，而在較小
we are known for our competence, and to a lesser extent depends on annual              程度上依賴於年度聘用費。相反，資產管理業務提供了長期穩
retainers. Asset management business on the contrary provides a long term              定的收入，並輔以根據基金表現所賺取的績效報酬。我們的團
steady income supplemented by carried interest based on the performance of             隊規模不斷擴大，並有望繼續協商管理更多基金。證券經紀業
funds. Our team increased and expects to continue to negotiate additional funds        務於二零二四年實現收支平衡後，並在二零二五年透過優化營
under management. Securities broking business is expected to remain viable after       運及業務表現進一步提升，我們預期證券經紀業務仍將保持可
achieving break-even in 2024, with further optimization of operations and business     行性。保證金融資業務於二零二五年啟動，將為客戶提供更大
performance in 2025. The activation of margin financing business in 2025 will offer    的靈活性，使客戶能夠發揮投資效益。在本地經濟疲軟的情況
clients greater flexibility by allowing them to leverage their investments. Money      下，放債業務將繼續謹慎行事。
lending business will continue to tread cautiously amidst the soft local economy.

PERMITTED INDEMNITY PROVISION                                                              獲准許之彌償條文

The Articles of Association provides that every Director shall be entitled to be           根據組織章程細則，本公司每名董事在其執行職責或其他方面
indemnified out of the assets of the Company against all losses or liabilities which he    與此有關之情況下可能蒙受或產生之所有損失或責任，有權獲
or she may sustain or incur in or about the execution of the duties of his or her office   得從本公司之資產中撥付彌償。此外，本公司已就董事可能面
otherwise in relation thereto. In addition, the Company has maintained appropriate         對之有關法律行動安排適當的董事及高級職員責任保險。
directors and officers liability insurance in respect of relevant legal actions against
the Directors.

CORPORATE GOVERNANCE                                                                      企業管治

The board of Directors of Da Yu Financial Holdings Limited (the “Company”) (the           大禹金融控股有限公司（「本公司」）董事會（「董事會」）深信企
“Board”) believes that corporate governance is essential to the success of the            業管治為本公司成功的關鍵，並已採取各項措施，以確保維持
Company and has adopted various measures to ensure that a high standard of                高標準的企業管治。本公司已應用香港聯合交易所有限公司
corporate governance is maintained. The Company has applied the principles               （「聯交所」）證券上市規則（「上市規則」）附錄C1所載之企業管
(the “Principles”) and adopted code provisions (the “Code Provisions”) set out in         治守則（「企管守則」）的原則（「原則」）及採納守則條文（「守則
the Corporate Governance Code (the “CG Code”) in Appendix C1 to the Rules                 條文」）為其企業管治常規。本公司將定期檢討及更新現行的常
Governing the Listing of Securities (the “Listing Rules”) on The Stock Exchange           規，以追隨企業管治的最新發展。
of Hong Kong Limited (the “Stock Exchange”) as its own code on corporate
governance practices. The current practices will be reviewed and updated regularly
to follow the latest practices in corporate governance.


The Company has complied with the Principles and the Code Provisions that were            本公司截至二零二五年十二月三十一日止年度內已遵守當時生
in force as set out in the CG Code during the year ended 31 December 2025.                效之企管守則所載原則及守則條文。

     the Chairman and the Managing Director.

Corporate Governance Functions                                                              企業管治職能

The Board is also responsible for performing the corporate governance duties as             董事會亦須根據企管守則之規定履行企業管治責任，並已就其

–    to develop and review the Company’s policies and practices on corporate                －   制定及檢討本公司企業管治政策及常規；
     governance;


–    to review and monitor the training and continuous professional development of          －   檢討及監察董事及高級管理人員之培訓及持續專業發展；
     Directors and senior management;

     with legal and regulatory requirements;                                                    常規；

     applicable to employees and Directors; and                                                 手冊；及

     Corporate Governance Report.                                                               披露。

DIVERSITY POLICY                                                                           多元化政策

Board Diversity                                                                            董事會多元化

The board diversity policy of the Company sets out the approach with the objective         本公司董事會多元化政策載有達致董事會多元化的宗旨及方
of achieving diversity on the Board. The Company is committed to the benefits              法。本公司致力於使董事會具備適當的專業知識、技能、經驗
of having the Board with appropriate balance of expertise, skills, experience and          及多樣的觀點角度。提名委員會代表董事會檢討及評估董事會
diversity of perspectives. The nomination committee reviews and assesses the               的組成，並建議任命新董事（如需要）。董事會的所有任命均基
Board composition on behalf of the Board and recommends the appointment of                 於精英管理，並將根據客觀標準考慮候選人，同時充分考慮董
new Directors, if necessary. All Board appointments will be based on meritocracy,          事會多元化的好處。
and candidates will be considered against objective criteria, having due regard for
the benefits of diversity on the Board.


The maintenance of the Board with a diversity of Directors will be based on a              維持董事會成員多元化將按一系列多樣的觀點與角度為基準，
range of diversity perspectives, including but not limited to the Company’s needs,         包括但不限於本公司的需求、性別、年齡、文化及教育背景、
gender, age, cultural and educational background, ethnicity, integrity, management         種族、誠信、管理經驗、技術技能、行業或專業知識及經驗、
experience, technical skills, industry or professional knowledge and experience,           任期、其他上市╱公眾公司的董事職務數目及其他重大外部事

The Company’s dividend policy aims to set out the approach adopted by the                   本公司之股息政策旨在闡明董事會就建議分配股息所採納的方

Company’s profits and for the Company to retain adequate reserves for future                儲備以用於未來的增長。
growth.


Subject to the requirement of the memorandum and articles of association of the             受限於本公司組織章程大綱及細則的要求、香港及開曼群島適
Company, the applicable laws and regulations of Hong Kong and the Cayman                    用的法律及法規以及適用於本公司的任何其他法律及法規，並

required, the approval of the Shareholders, the Company shall pay annual dividends          集團並無重大投資或承擔，則應向股東派付全年股息。考慮到
to the Shareholders if the Group is profitable, operation environment is stable             包括但不限於本集團當前及未來的運作、財務狀況、發展途
and there is no significant investment or commitment made by the Group. The                 徑、當前的經濟環境、合同限制、資本及其他儲備要求、從本
aggregate amount per year shall not exceed 30% of the consolidated annual net               公司的附屬公司及聯營公司收取的股息，以及董事會認為相關
profits (excluding extraordinary items, if any) attributable to the Shareholders, taking    的任何其他條件或因素並考慮到董事的受信責任等標準，每年

environment, contractual restrictions, capital and other reserve requirements,              息政策允許本公司除全年股息外，可不時宣派特別股息。
dividends received from the Company’s subsidiaries and associates, as well as any
other conditions or factors which the Board deems relevant and having regard to

APPROACH AND STRATEGY                                                                    方針及策略

Da Yu Financial Holdings Limited (the “Company”) believes in delivering long term,       大禹金融控股有限公司（「本公司」）相信要為股東創造長期及
sustainable value creation to our shareholders. In doing so, we recognise that the       可持續的價值。於此過程中，我們明白到，我們所作的選擇將
choices we make will have an impact on the communities in which we carry on our          對我們開展業務的社區產生影響，因此我們應令此影響成為正
business and we should make such impact a positive one. We are committed to              面。我們致力促進社會和環境的可持續發展，並努力將該等原
contributing to the sustainable development of society and the environment, and          則作為我們實踐及管治的一部分。本公司於二零一九年十一月
endeavour to embrace these principles as part of our practice and governance. The        採用一套可持續發展政策，其中包括我們於就業及勞工實務、
Company has adopted a Sustainability Policy in November 2019 which covers our            商業誠信、環境及社會各方面之原則。
principles in the area of employment and labour practices, business integrity, the
environment and the community.


This report outlines the sustainability initiatives and Key Performance Indicators       本報告概述本公司及其附屬公司（「本集團」或「我們」）的可持
(“KPI”) of the Company and its subsidiaries (the “Group” or “we”) that are material to   續發展舉措及關鍵績效指標（「關鍵績效指標」），這些指標截至
the Group and its stakeholders on Environmental, Social and Governance (“ESG”)           二零二五年十二月三十一日止年度在環境、社會及管治（「環社
issues for the year ended 31 December 2025. This report supplements information          管」）議題方面對本集團及其持份者具有重要意義。本報告是對
disclosed in this Annual Report.                                                         本年報內披露資料的補充。


The subject areas and aspects are presented in accordance with the                       所涉範疇及層面乃根據香港聯合交易所有限公司（「聯交所」）證
“Environmental, Social and Governance Reporting Code” (the “ESG Reporting                券上市規則附錄C2所載之環境、社會及管治報告守則（「環社
Code”) set out in Appendix C2 to the Rules Governing the Listing of Securities on        管報告守則」）而編製。
The Stock Exchange of Hong Kong Limited (the “Stock Exchange”).


GOVERNANCE STRUCTURE                                                                     管治架構

The board of Directors of the Company (the “Board”) is well aware of its role and        本公司董事會（「董事會」）深知其在監督環社管事項，包括制定
responsibility in oversight of ESG matters, such as setting up ESG-related goals         環社管相關目標以及評估及管理重大環境及社會風險事宜方面
and targets as well as assessment and management of material environmental               之角色及責任。環社管工作由本公司管理層執行，當中包括本
and social risks issues. The ESG works are implemented by the management                 公司執行董事。管理層負責確定環社管相關風險，並為本集團
of the Company which consists of the Executive Directors of the Company. The             判斷適當風險範圍。建立了系統及程序以確定、評估及管理業
management is responsible for identifying ESG-related risks and determining              務及活動之風險，包括環社管相關風險。管理層每年向本公司
the appropriate risk profile for the Group. Systems and procedures are put               審核委員會及董事會提交調查結果以供審閱，詳情載於本年報
in place to identify, evaluate and manage the risks of business and activities,          「企業管治報告」內「風險管理及內部監控」一節。

     C. CLIMATE CHANGE                                                                         C. 氣候變化

          The Group recognises that climate-related issues can materially impact our            本集團明白，氣候相關議題可能對我們的業務營運及長
          business operations and long-term value. As a financial service provider, we are      期價值產生重大影響。作為金融服務提供商，我們致力
          committed to integrating climate change considerations into our core strategy         於將氣候變化因素納入核心策略，並加強氣候相關風
          and enhancing climate-related risk assessments to fully evaluate the material         險評估，以全面評估其對我們業務及投資組合的重大影
          impacts on our business and our investment portfolios. The Group discloses            響。本集團依據環社管報告守則，以四大支柱框架（管
          climate-related information based on a four-pillar framework (Governance,             治、策略、風險管理、指標及目標）披露氣候相關資料。
          Strategy, Risk Management, and Metrics & Targets) in compliance with the ESG
          Reporting Code.

Strategy                                                                                   策略

The Group aims to enhance our business resilience to climate change and build              本集團致力於增強業務應對氣候變化的韌性，並打造環
an environmentally responsible enterprise. We have refined our risk assessment             境友善企業。我們已完善風險評估流程，分析氣候變化
procedures to analyze the potential impacts on our businesses and value chain,             對業務及價值鏈的潛在影響，從而評估並制定最佳緩解
thereby evaluating and formulating optimal mitigation measures.                            措施。


To operationalize these goals and address the limitations of traditional risk              為實現該等目標並解決傳統風險評估流程在捕捉長期氣
assessment procedures in capturing long-term climate impacts, the Group                    候影響方面的局限性，本集團截至二零二五年十二月
conducted our first comprehensive climate scenario analysis during the year                三十一日止年度進行首次全面的氣候情景分析。該分析
ended 31 December 2025. This analysis covered both physical risks and                      涵蓋實體風險及轉型風險與機遇，並對每類風險與機遇
transition risks, as well as opportunities, with detailed screening and assessment         進行詳細的篩選及評估。
performed for each risk and opportunity category.


To conduct a comprehensive analysis of climate-related risks and opportunities             為全面分析業務發展過程中可能出現的氣候相關風險與
that may arise throughout our business development, the Group needs to take                機遇，本集團需要考慮全球氣溫上升路徑、氣候政策變
into account factors such as global temperature rise pathways, changes in                  化及影響的時間範圍等因素。因此，我們從聯合國政府
climate policies, and time horizons of impact. Therefore, we selected scenario             間氣候變化專門委員會（IPCC）        （ AR6）
                                                                                                          《 第六次評估報告》
assumption models and relevant parameters from the Sixth Assessment Report                 及央行與監管機構綠色金融網絡（NGFS）第五階段公開報
(AR6) of the Intergovernmental Panel on Climate Change (IPCC) and the Phase                告中選取情景假設模型及相關參數。所選情景符合本集
5 public release of the Network for Greening the Financial System (NGFS). The              團的行業性質及策略目標，並與中國的「雙碳」戰略及香
scenarios selected align with the nature of the Group’s industry and strategic             港政府「二零五零年前實現碳中和」的目標一致。
goals, and is consistent with China’s “dual carbon” strategy and the Hong Kong
government’s target of “achieving carbon neutrality by 2050”.


In the scenario analysis, we assume that there will be no changes to our                   在情景分析中，我們假設在預測風險影響範圍內，我們
mitigation policies and reporting scope within the anticipated impact range of             的緩解政策及報告範圍不會改變。下表呈列選定的氣候
risks. The following table presents the selected climate scenario models covering          情景模型，涵蓋低排放及高排放情景，以便更好地了解
both low-emission and high-emission scenarios to better understand the                     氣候變化對本集團的影響。
impacts of climate change on the Group.


 Scope                                                 Same as the reporting boundary, which covers all principal operating activities of the Company,
                                                       Yu Ming, YMHD Fund, Morton Securities and Morgan Finance.
 範圍                                                    與匯報範圍相同，涵蓋本公司、禹銘、YMHD基金、萬基證券及萬基財務的所有主營業務。
 Time Horizons                                         Short-term (up to 2030), medium-term (up to 2040), and long-term (up to 2050). These are

                                                                  IPCC for physical risks analysis:
                                                                  IPCC實體風險分析：
                                                                   • SSP 1-2.6: Global warming reaches 2.0 ºC. Governments’social, economic and clean
                                                                     energy transitions align with historical trends. Stringent policies amplify transition risks for
                                                                     enterprises, while physical risks remain notable.
                                                                   • SSP 1-2.6：全球暖化達到2.0℃。各國政府的社會、經濟及清潔能源轉型與歷史趨勢相
                                                                     符。嚴格的政策加劇企業面臨的轉型風險，而實體風險仍然顯著。
                                                                   • SSP 5-8.5: Global warming exceeds 4 ºC. Delayed government climate action, stalled
                                                                     emission reduction/adaptation and insufficient policies drive extreme climate impacts,
                                                                     increasing enterprises’immediate and long-term physical risks.
                                                                   • SSP 5-8.5：全球暖化超過4℃。政府氣候行動遲緩、減排╱調適停滯不前以及政策不足導
                                                                     致極端氣候影響加劇，增加企業面臨的短期及長期實體風險。
            Scenario Used
                                                                  NGFS for transition risks and opportunities analysis:
            所用情景
                                                                  NGFS轉型風險與機遇分析：
                                                                   • Net Zero 2050: Early adoption of stringent climate policies. By reducing energy demand
                                                                     and advancing low-carbon technologies, it aims to limit global warming to well below 1.5 ºC
                                                                     and achieve global net-zero carbon dioxide emissions around 2050.
                                                                   • 二零五零年淨零排放：儘早採取嚴格的氣候政策。透過降低能源需求及推進低碳技術，該
                                                                     計劃旨在將全球暖化幅度控制在遠低於1.5℃，並在二零五零年左右實現全球二氧化碳淨
                                                                     零排放。
                                                                   • Current Policies: Only currently implemented climate policies remain in place, leading to
                                                                     continued growth in GHG emissions. Global warming is projected to exceed 3 ºC, resulting
                                                                     in severe physical risks.
                                                                   • 現行政策：目前僅保留現有的氣候政策，導致溫室氣體排放持續增長。預計全球暖化將超
                                                                     過3℃，造成嚴重的實體風險。


          The Group recognises that integrating both quantitative and qualitative analysis              本集團明白，整合定量及定性分析能夠更全面地評估氣
          would deliver a more comprehensive assessment of climate-related risks and                    候相關風險與機遇。然而，由於相關營運數據分散在各
          opportunities. However, the Group cannot reliably compile various cross-                      個業務單元，且業界認可的此類指標衡量方法仍存在高
          industry metrics for these climate issues in a reasonable and cost-effective                  度不確定性，本集團無法以合理且具成本效益的方式可
          manner, as relevant operational data is fragmented across business units and                  靠地編製各種跨行業的氣候議題指標。此外，本集團的
          industry-accepted measurement methodologies for such metrics remain highly                    氣候相關行動已完全融入日常業務營運，並無單獨劃撥
          uncertain. Besides, the Group’s climate-related actions are fully embedded                    專款用於應對氣候相關風險與機遇。有關營運整合進一
          within day-to-day business operations, with no separately identifiable funds                  步導致本集團無法提供該等氣候舉措當前及預測財務影
          earmarked specifically for addressing climate-related risks and opportunities.                響的量化數據。
          This operational integration further prevents the Group from providing
          quantitative data on the current and anticipated financial impacts of these climate
          initiatives.

          climate risks and opportunities and provided detailed qualitative disclosures                 務影響，並在下表中提供詳細的定性披露。我們亦積極
          in the tables below. We are also actively enhancing internal data integration                 提升內部數據整合系統及情景建模能力，以逐步提高氣
          systems and scenario-modeling capabilities to gradually improve the granularity               候定量披露的精細度，並承諾，倘我們的營運策略作出
          of quantitative climate disclosures, and commit to promptly disclosing the                    重大調整，我們將及時披露其對財務狀況、經營業績及
          quantitative impacts on our financial position, operating performance, and cash               現金流量的定量影響。

          Risk Management                                                                               風險管理

          The Group has integrated the processes for identifying, assessing, prioritizing,              本集團已將識別、評估、優先排序及管理氣候相關風險
          and managing climate-related risks and opportunities into the overall risk                    與機遇的流程整合到整體風險管理框架及系統中，從而
          management framework and system to embed climate risk management into                         將氣候風險管理融入日常營運。我們亦確保本集團有效
          day-to-day operations. We also ensure that the Group effectively addresses the                應對氣候變化帶來的挑戰。此整合與我們現有的風險管
          challenges posed by climate change. This integration aligns with our existing                 理架構相符，截至二零二五年十二月三十一日止年度，
          risk management structure and no significant changes were made to our risk                    我們的風險管理流程並無重大變更。在整個流程中，本
          management process during the year ended 31 December 2025. Throughout                         集團會考慮資產位置及類型、歷史極端天氣風險以及能
          the processes, the Group considers parameters such as asset location and                      源消耗模式等參數。我們將利用來自公開可得情景資料
          type, historical exposure to extreme weather and energy consumption patterns.                 來源的氣候數據，以及水電消耗記錄及營運事故日誌等
          Climate data from the publicly available scenario sources, as well as internal data           內部數據。以下為本集團氣候風險與機遇管理流程的概
          such as utility consumption records and operational incident logs are utilized.               要：
          Below is the summary of the climate risk and opportunity management process
          of the Group:


            1.   Identification                                   The Group conducts research on climate change trends, domestic and international industry
                                                                  developments, and technological changes, carries out peer benchmarking, and collects
                                                                  stakeholders’ opinions. We also perform climate-related scenario analysis, taking into
                                                                  account our operational conditions and business characteristics. These efforts enable the
                                                                  comprehensive and objective gathering of insights on potential climate risks and opportunities,
                                                                  ensuring the resulting list of climate risks and opportunities covers key links throughout the
                                                                  entire business process.
            1.   識別                                               本集團進行氣候變化趨勢、國內外產業發展及技術變革的研究、進行同儕標竿分析，並收集
                                                                  持分者意見。我們亦結合自身的營運狀況與業務特點，進行氣候相關情景分析。該等努力有
                                                                  助於全面、客觀地收集有關潛在氣候風險與機遇的洞察，確保最終形成的氣候風險與機遇清
                                                                  單涵蓋整個業務流程的關鍵環節。
            2.   Evaluation                                       The Group conducts a comprehensive assessment, analyzing the potential impacts of climate-
                                                                  related risks and opportunities on our business model, value chain, and financial performance,
                                                                  as well as assessing their likelihood of occurrence and corresponding magnitude of impact.
            2.   評估                                               本集團進行全面評估，分析氣候相關風險與機遇對我們的業務模式、價值鏈及財務表現的潛
                                                                  在影響，並評估其發生的可能性及相應的影響程度。
            3.   Prioritization                                   Based on the assessment results of climate-related risks and opportunities, the Group
                                                                  prioritizes the identified items based on the likelihood of occurrence and significance of impact.
                                                                  This prioritization process aligns with our overall risk management framework and business
                                                                  objectives.
            3.   優先排序                                             基於氣候相關風險與機遇的評估結果，本集團根據風險發生的可能性及影響嚴重程度對已識
                                                                  別事項進行優先排序。此優先排序過程與我們的整體風險管理框架及業務目標一致。
            4.   Monitoring                                       The management regularly evaluates and monitors the identified climate-related risks and
                                                                  opportunities, as well as the effectiveness of corresponding mitigation and response measures.
                                                                  Meanwhile, the management periodically reports the results of identifying, assessing, and
                                                                  managing climate-related risks and opportunities to the Audit Committee and the Board. This
                                                                  strengthens the Board’s oversight of risk and opportunities management and ensures the
                                                                  effective implementation of risk response measures and opportunity capture initiatives.
            4.   監測                                               管理層定期評估及監測已識別的氣候相關風險與機遇，以及相應緩解與因應措施的成效。同
                                                                  時，管理層定期向審核委員會及董事會報告識別、評估及管理氣候相關風險與機遇的結果。
                                                                  此有助於加強董事會對風險與機遇管理的監督，並確保風險因應措施及機遇把握舉措的有效
                                                                  實施。

Reasonable assurance is a high level of assurance, but is not a guarantee that             合理保證是高水平的保證，但不能保證按照香港審計準則進行
an audit conducted in accordance with HKSAs will always detect a material                  的審計，在某一重大錯誤陳述存在時總能發現。錯誤陳述可以
misstatement when it exists. Misstatements can arise from fraud or error and are           由欺詐或錯誤引起，如果合理預期它們單獨或滙總起來可能影
considered material if, individually or in the aggregate, they could reasonably be         響綜合財務報表使用者依賴綜合財務報表所作出的經濟決定，
expected to influence the economic decisions of users taken on the basis of these          則有關的錯誤陳述可被視作重大。
consolidated financial statements.


As part of an audit in accordance with HKSAs, we exercise professional judgement           在根據香港審計準則進行審計的過程中，吾等運用了專業判
and maintain professional skepticism throughout the audit. We also:                        斷，保持了專業懷疑態度。吾等亦：


•     identify and assess the risks of material misstatement of the consolidated           •   識別和評估由於欺詐或錯誤而導致綜合財務報表存在重
      financial statements, whether due to fraud or error, design and perform                  大錯誤陳述的風險，設計及執行審計程序以應對這些風
      audit procedures responsive to those risks, and obtain audit evidence that               險，以及獲取充足和適當的審計憑證，作為吾等意見的
      is sufficient and appropriate to provide a basis for our opinion. The risk of            基礎。由於欺詐可能涉及串謀、偽造、蓄意遺漏、虛假
      not detecting a material misstatement resulting from fraud is higher than for            陳述，或凌駕於內部控制之上，因此未能發現因欺詐而
      one resulting from error, as fraud may involve collusion, forgery, intentional           導致的重大錯誤陳述的風險高於未能發現因錯誤而導致
      omissions, misrepresentations, or the override of internal control.                      的重大錯誤陳述的風險。


•     obtain an understanding of internal control relevant to the audit in order to        •   了解與審計相關的內部控制，以設計適當的審計程序，
      design audit procedures that are appropriate in the circumstances, but not               但目的並非對    貴集團內部控制的有效性發表意見。
      for the purpose of expressing an opinion on the effectiveness of the Group’s
      internal control.


•     evaluate the appropriateness of accounting policies used and the                     •   評價董事所採用會計政策的恰當性及作出會計估計和相
      reasonableness of accounting estimates and related disclosures made by the               關披露的合理性。
      directors.


•     conclude on the appropriateness of the directors’ use of the going concern           •   對董事採用持續經營會計基礎的恰當性作出結論。根據
      basis of accounting and, based on the audit evidence obtained, whether                   所獲取的審計憑證，確定是否存在與事項或情況有關的
      a material uncertainty exists related to events or conditions that may cast              重大不確定性，從而可能導致對      貴集團的持續經營能
      significant doubt on the Group’s ability to continue as a going concern. If we           力產生重大疑慮。如果吾等認為存在重大不確定性，則
      conclude that a material uncertainty exists, we are required to draw attention           有必要在核數師報告中提請使用者注意綜合財務報表中
      in our auditor’s report to the related disclosures in the consolidated financial         的相關披露。假若有關的披露不足，則吾等應當發表非
      statements or, if such disclosures are inadequate, to modify our opinion. Our            無保留意見。吾等的結論是基於核數師報告日止所取得
      conclusions are based on the audit evidence obtained up to the date of our               的審計憑證。然而，未來事項或情況可能導致        貴集團
      auditor’s report. However, future events or conditions may cause the Group               不能持續經營。
      to cease to continue as a going concern.


•     evaluate the overall presentation, structure and content of the consolidated         •   評價綜合財務報表的整體列報方式、結構和內容，包括
      financial statements, including the disclosures, and whether the consolidated            披露，以及綜合財務報表是否中肯反映交易和事項。
      financial statements represent the underlying transactions and events in a
      manner that achieves fair presentation.


•     plan and perform the group audit to obtain sufficient appropriate audit              •   計劃及執行集團審核，以就     貴集團內實體或業務單位
      evidence regarding the financial information of the entities or business units           的財務信息獲取充足及適當的審計憑證，作為對集團財
      within the group as a basis for forming an opinion on the group financial                務報表形成意見的基礎。吾等負責指導、監督及審閱為

          accordance with HKFRS Accounting Standards, which collective                       財務報告準則（其包括香港財務報告準則會計準

          financial statements include the applicable disclosure provisions of the

          of these consolidated financial statements are set out below.                      於下文。

          the historical cost basis except for financial instruments classified              按公平值計入損益的財務資產的金融工具按公平
          as financial assets at FVPL which are stated at fair values. The                   值列賬除外。計量基準於下文會計政策內詳述。
          measurement bases are fully described in the accounting policies
          below.


          Historical cost is generally based on the fair value of the consideration          歷史成本通常是基於交換貨品及服務所付代價之
          given in exchange for goods and services.                                          公平值。


          It should be noted that accounting estimates and assumptions are                   務請注意，編製綜合財務報表時須採用會計估計
          used in preparation of the consolidated financial statements. Although             及假設。儘管此等估計乃按管理層對現行事件及
          these estimates are based on management’s best knowledge and                       行動所深知及判斷而作出，惟實際結果最終或會
          judgement of current events and actions, actual results may ultimately             有別於該等估計。涉及較高程度判斷或複雜性之
          differ from those estimates. The areas involving a higher degree of                範圍或於綜合財務報表作出重大假設及估計之範

             3.2 Basis of consolidation                                                             3.2 綜合之基準

                    The consolidated financial statements comprise the financial                      綜合財務報表包括本公司及其附屬公司之財務報
                    statements of the Company and its subsidiaries.                                   表。


                    All intra-group transactions, balances and unrealised gains and losses            集團各成員公司之間之所有集團內部交易、結餘
                    on transactions between group companies are eliminated in preparing               及未變現收益及虧損均於編製綜合財務報表時對
                    the consolidated financial statements.                                            銷。


             3.3 Subsidiaries                                                                       3.3 附屬公司

                    Subsidiaries are investees over which the Group is able to exercise               附屬公司為本集團可行使控制權之被投資方。倘
                    control. The Group controls investees if all three of the following               以下三個因素全部滿足時，本集團控制被投資
                    elements are present: power over the investees, exposure, or rights,              方：對被投資方之權力，來自被投資方可變回報
                    to variable returns from the investees, and the ability to use its power          之風險或權利及利用其權力影響該等可變回報之
                    to affect those variable returns. Control is reassessed whenever facts            能力。當有事實或情況顥示任何該等控制因素可
                    and circumstances indicate that there may be a change in any of these             能出現變動時，控制權會被重新評估。
                    elements of control.


                    In the Company’s statement of financial position, investment in a                 在本公司財務狀況表中，於附屬公司之投資以成
                    subsidiary is carried at cost less any impairment loss (Note 3.13).               本減任何減值虧損（附註3.13)入賬。本公司按截
                    The results of subsidiaries are accounted for by the Company on                   至報告日期已收及應收股息作為附屬公司之業績
                    the basis of dividends received and receivable at the reporting date.             入賬。所有已收取股息不論來自投資對象之收購
                    All dividends whether received out of the investee’s pre or post-                 前或後溢利，均於本公司之損益內確認。
                    acquisition profits are recognised in the Company’s profit or loss.

         assets and liabilities on the acquisition date.                                     量。


         Goodwill is measured at cost less impairment losses. For the purpose                商譽乃按成本減去減值虧損計量。就減值測試而

         or groups of assets. A CGU to which goodwill has been allocated is                  年透過將其賬面值與其可收回金額作比較而進行
         tested for impairment annually, by comparing its carrying amount with               減值測試及於有跡象顯示該單位可能出現減值時

             3.7 Leases                                                                                  3.7 租賃

                    The Group as lessee                                                                     集團作為承租人

                    All leases are capitalised in the consolidated statement of financial                   所有租賃須於綜合財務狀況報表內撥充資本作為
                    position as right-of-use assets and lease liabilities.                                  使用權資產及租賃負債。


                    Right-of-use asset                                                                      使用權資產

                    The right-of-use asset is recognised at cost and would comprise: (i)                    使用權資產按成本確認，並將包括：(i)初始計量
                    the amount of the initial measurement of the lease liability (see below                 租賃負債之金額（見下文有關租賃負債入賬之會
                    for the accounting policy to account for lease liability); (ii) any lease               計政策）；(ii)於開始日期或之前支付之任何租賃
                    payments made at or before the commencement date, less any lease                        付款減去任何已收租賃優惠；(iii)承租人產生之任
                    incentives received; (iii) any initial direct costs incurred by the lessee;             何初始直接成本；及(iv)承租人拆除及移除相關
                    and (iv) an estimate of costs to be incurred by the lessee in dismantling               資產以符合租賃條款及條件所規定情況時將產生
                    and removing the underlying asset to the condition required by the                      之估計成本。初步確認後，本集團應用成本模型
                    terms and conditions of the lease. Subsequent to initial recognition,                   計量使用權資產。根據成本模型，本集團按成本
                    the Group measures the right-of-use assets applying a cost model.                       減去任何累計折舊及任何減值虧損計量使用權資
                    Under the cost model, the Group measures the right-of-use at cost,                      產，並就租賃負債之任何重新計量作出調整。使
                    less any accumulated depreciation and any impairment losses, and                        用權資產按租期以直線攤分法折舊。
                    adjusted for any remeasurement of lease liability. Right-of-use assets
                    are depreciated on a straight-line basis over the lease terms.


                    Lease liability                                                                         租賃負債

                    The lease liability is recognised at the present value of the lease                     租賃負債按於租賃開始日期尚未支付之租賃款項
                    payments that are not paid at the date of commencement of the lease.                    現值確認。如可即時釐定租賃隱含之利率，則租
                    The lease payments are discounted using the interest rate implicit in                   賃付款使用該利率貼現。如不可即時釐定該利
                    the lease, if that rate can be readily determined. If that rate cannot                  率，則本集團將使用其增量借貸利率貼現。
                    be readily determined, the Group uses the Group’s incremental
                    borrowing rate.


                    Subsequent to the commencement date, the Group measures the                             於開始日期後，本集團將以下列方式計量租賃負
                    lease liability by: (i) increasing the carrying amount to reflect interest on           債：(i)增加賬面值以反映租賃負債利息；(ii)減少
                    the lease liability; (ii) reducing the carrying amount to reflect the lease             賬面值以反映已作出之租賃付款；及(iii)重新計
                    payments made; and (iii) remeasuring the carrying amount to reflect                     量賬面值以反映任何重新評估或租賃修訂，例如
                    any reassessment or lease modifications, e.g., a change in future                       某一指數或比率改變、租期改變、實質固定租賃
                    lease payments arising from change in an index or rate, a change in                     付款改變或對於購買相關資產之評估改變令未來
                    the lease term, a change in the in-substance fixed lease payments or                    租賃付款改變。
                    a change in assessment to purchase the underlying asset.


                    When the Group renegotiates the contractual terms of a lease with the                   當本集團與出租人重新協商租賃合約條款時，如
                    lessor, where the renegotiated increases the scope of the lease that                    果重新協商擴大了租賃範圍，且不作為單獨租賃
                    should not be accounted for as a separate lease (whether that is an                     入賬（無論是延長租賃期），則採用修改日適用的
                    extension to the lease term), the lease liability is remeasured using the               折現率重新計量租賃負債，使用權資產也進行相
                    discount rate applicable on the modification date, with the right-of-use                同金額的調整。
                    asset being adjusted by the same amount.

                    Impairment loss on financial assets                                               財務資產之減值虧損

                    The Group recognises loss allowances for expected credit loss (“ECL”)             本集團就貿易應收款項、按揭貸款、有期貸款、
                    on trade receivables, mortgage loans, term loans, contract assets and             合約資產及按攤銷成本計量的財務資產的預期信
                    financial assets measured at amortised cost. The ECLs are measured                貸虧損（「預期信貸虧損」）確認虧損撥備。預期信
                    on either of the following bases: (1) 12-month ECL: these are the ECLs            貸虧損將採用以下基準計量：(1) 12個月預期信
                    that result from possible default events within the 12 months after               貸虧損：指報告日期後12個月內可能發生的違
                    the reporting date; and (2) lifetime ECL: these are ECLs that result              約事件而導致的預期信貸虧損；及(2)全期預期
                    from all possible default events over the expected life of a financial            信貸虧損：指金融工具的預計年期內所有可能發
                    instrument. The maximum period considered when estimating ECLs is                 生的違約事件而導致的預期信貸虧損。於估計預
                    the maximum contractual period over which the Group is exposed to                 期信貸虧損時所考慮的最長期間為本集團面臨信
                    credit risk.                                                                      貸風險的最長合約期限。


                    ECLs are a probability-weighted estimate of credit losses. Credit                 預期信貸虧損為信貸虧損的概率加權估計。信貸
                    losses are measured as the difference between all contractual cash                虧損乃按本集團根據合約應付的所有合約現金流
                    flows that are due to the Group in accordance with the contract and               量與本集團預期收取的所有現金流量之間的差額
                    all the cash flows that the Group expects to receive. The shortfall is            計量。該差額其後按資產原有實際利率相近的利
                    then discounted at an approximation to the assets’ original effective             率貼現。
                    interest rate.


                    The Group measured loss allowances for trade receivables, contract                本集團根據香港財務報告準則第9號「金融工具」
                    assets and amount due from a related company that are trade in                    簡化法計量貿易應收款項、合約資產及貿易性質
                    nature using HKFRS 9 “Financial Instruments” simplified approach                  之應收一間關聯公司款項的虧損撥備，並已根據
                    and has calculated ECLs based on lifetime ECL. The Group has                      全期預期信貸虧損計算預期信貸虧損。本集團已
                    established a provision matrix that is based on the Group’s historical            設立根據本集團過往信貸虧損經驗計算之撥備矩
                    credit loss experience, adjusted for forward-looking factors specific to          陣，並按債務人特定之前瞻性因素及經濟環境作
                    the debtors and the economic environment.                                         出調整。


                    For mortgage loans, term loans and other debt financial assets,                   就按揭貸款、有期賃款及其他債務財務資產而
                    the ECLs are based on the 12-month ECL. However, when there                       言，預期信貸虧損乃根據12個月預期信貸虧損
                    has been a significant increase in credit risk since origination, the             計算。然而，倘信貸風險自產生以來顯著增加，
                    allowance will be based on the lifetime ECL.                                      則撥備將根據全期預期信貸虧損計算。

         (i)   Significant increase in credit risk                                           (i)   信貨風險顯著增加

               When determining whether the credit risk of a financial                             當釐定財務資產之信貸風險自初步確認以
               asset has increased significantly since initial recognition and                     來是否有大幅增加及於估計預期信貸虧損
               when estimating ECL, the Group considers reasonable and                             時，本集團考慮相關且無需付出過多成本
               supportable information that is relevant and available without                      或努力便可取得之合理及有根據資料。這
               undue cost or effort. This includes both quantitative and                           包括根據本集團之過往經驗及已知信貸評
               qualitative information analysis, based on the Group’s historical                   估作出的定量及定性資料分析，並包括前
               experience and informed credit assessment and including                             瞻性資料。
               forward-looking information.


               In particular, the following information is taken into account                      具體而言，評估信貸風險曾否顯著增加時
               when assessing whether credit risk has increased significantly:                     考慮下列資料：


               •      an actual or expected significant deterioration in the                       •    金融工具的外部（如有）或內部信貸
                      financial instrument’s external (if available) or internal                        評級實際或預期嚴重轉差；
                      credit rating;


               •      significant deterioration in external market indicators                      •    外部市場信貸風險指標嚴重轉差，
                      of credit risk, e.g. a significant increase in the credit                         如信貸息差、債務人的信貸違約掉
                      spread, the credit default swap prices for the debtor;                            期價格大幅上升；


               •      existing or forecast adverse changes in (1) the                              •    (1)監管、經濟或技術環境；(2)業務
                      regulatory, economic or technological environments;                               或財務狀況的現存或預計不利變動
                      (2) business or financial conditions that are expected                            預期會導致債務人履行其債務責任
                      to cause a significant decrease in the debtor’s ability to                        的能力大幅下降；
                      meet its debt obligations;


               •      an actual or expected significant deterioration in the                       •    債務人的經營業績實際或預期嚴重
                      operating results of the debtor; or                                               轉差；或


               •      financial re-organisation/restructuring entered by the                       •    債務人需進行財務重整╱重組。
                      debtors.


               The Group assumes that the credit risk on a financial asset has                     倘財務資產逾期30日以上，本集團假設
               increased significantly if it is more than 30 days past due.                        財務資產的信貸風險大幅增加。


               The Group regularly monitors the effectiveness of the criteria                      本集團定期監察用以確定信貸風險曾否顯
               used to identify whether there has been a significant increase                      著增加的標準的成效，並於適當時候作出
               in credit risk and revises them as appropriate to ensure that the                   修訂，從而確保有關標準能夠於款項逾期
               criteria are capable of identifying a significant increase in credit                前確定信貸風險顯著增加。
               risk before the amount becomes past due.

                    (ii)   Definition of default                                                         (ii)   違約之定義

                           For internal credit risk management, the Group considers an                          就內部信貸風險管理而言，本集團認為，
                           event of default occurs when information developed internally                        倘內部生成或自外部來源獲得之資料顯示
                           or obtained from external sources indicates that the debtor is                       債務人不太可能向其債權人（包括本集團）
                           unlikely to pay its creditors, including the Group, in full (without                 悉數付款（不考慮本集團持有之任何抵押
                           taking into account any collateral held by the Group).                               品），則發生違約事件。


                           Irrespective of the above, the Group considers that default has                      無論上述情形如何，本集團認為，倘財務
                           occurred when a financial asset is more than 90 days past                            資產逾期超過90日，則發生違約事件，
                           due unless the Group has reasonably supportable information                          除非本集團有合理可作為依據的資料說明
                           to demonstrate that a more lagging default criterion is more                         更寬鬆的違約標準更為合適，則作別論。
                           appropriate. In particular, the following qualitative indicators                     具體而言，釐定發生違約的風險時會計及
                           are taken into account in determining the risk of a default                          下列定量指標：
                           occurring:


                           •      probable bankruptcy entered by the borrowers; and                             •     借款人可能破產；及


                           •      death of the debtor.                                                          •     債務人身故。


                    (iii) Credit-impaired financial assets                                               (iii) 信貸減值財務資產

                           A financial asset is credit-impaired when one or more events of                      當發生一項或多項對財務資產估計未來現
                           default that have a detrimental impact on the estimated future                       金流量有不利影響之違約事件時，財務資
                           cash flows of that financial asset have occurred. Evidence that                      產出現信貸減值。財務資產信貸減值之證
                           a financial asset is credit-impaired includes observable data                        據包括以下可觀察事件：
                           about the following events:


                           (a)    significant financial difficulty of the issuer or the                         (a)   發行人或借款人陷入重大財務困
                                  borrower;                                                                           難；


                           (b)    a breach of contract, such as a default or past due                           (b)   違反合約，如違約或逾期事件；
                                  event;


                           (c)    the lenders of the borrower, for economic or contractual                      (c)   借款人之放款人因與借款人出現財
                                  reasons relating to the borrower’s financial difficulty,                            務困難有關之經濟或合約理由而給
                                  having granted to the borrower concessions that the                                 予借款人在一般情況下放款人不予
                                  lenders would not otherwise consider;                                               考慮之優惠條件；


                           (d)    probable bankruptcy or other financial reorganisation                         (d)   借款人有可能破產或進行其他財務
                                  entered by the borrower; or                                                         重組；或


                           (e)    the disappearance of an active market for that financial                      (e)   由於財務困難致使財務資產之活躍
                                  asset because of financial difficulties.                                            市場消失。

         (iv) Write-off policy                                                               (iv) 撇銷政策

               The Group writes off a financial asset when there is information                    當有資料顯示債務人存在嚴重財務困難且
               indicating that the debtor is in severe financial difficulty and                    沒有現實的收回前景時（例如對手方進入
               there is no realistic prospect of recovery, for example, when                       破產程序），本集團撇銷財務資產。根據
               the counterparty has entered into bankruptcy proceedings.                           法律建議（如適用），撇銷的財務資產可
               Financial assets written off may still be subject to enforcement                    能仍會根據本集團的追回程序進行法律行
               activities under the Group’s recovery procedures, taking into                       動。任何收回的款項均在損益中確認。
               account legal advice where appropriate. Any recoveries made
               are recognised in profit or loss.


         (v)   Measurement and recognition of ECL                                            (v)   預期信貸虧損的計量及確認

               Generally, the ECL is the difference between all contractual                        一般而言，預期信貸虧損為根據合約應付
               cash flows that are due to the Group in accordance with the                         本集團的所有合約現金流量與本集團預期
               contract and the cash flows that the Group expects to receive,                      就財務資產將收取的現金流量按初步確認
               discounted at the effective interest rate determined at initial                     時釐定的實際利率貼現的差額。
               recognition for financial assets.


               The measurement of ECL is a function of the probability of                          預期信貸虧損的計量為違約概率、違約損
               default, loss given default (i.e. the magnitude of the loss if there                失率（即違約時的損失程度）及違約風險的
               is a default) and the exposure at default. The assessment of the                    函數。違約概率及違約損失率的評估是依
               probability of default and loss given default is based on historical                據歷史數據及前瞻性資料而作出。預期信
               data and forward-looking information. Estimation of ECL reflects                    貸虧損的估算反映以各自發生違約的風險
               an unbiased and probability weighted amount that is determined                      作加權的公正及概率加權金額。
               with the respective risks of default occurring as the weights.


         (vi) Derecognition of financial assets                                              (vi) 停止確認財務資產

               The Group derecognises a financial asset when the contractual                       當與該財務資產有關的未來現金流量的合
               rights to the future cash flows in relation to the financial asset                  約權利到期時，或當該財務資產已轉移且
               expire or when the financial asset has been transferred and                         轉移符合香港財務報告準則第9號的取消
               the transfer meets the criteria for derecognition in accordance                     確認標準時，本集團取消確認該財務資
               with HKFRS 9. All regular way sales of financial assets are                         產。所有常規出售之財務資產於交易日
               derecognised on the trade date, that is, the date that the Group                    （即本集團承諾出售該資產之日期）取消
               commits to sell the asset.                                                          確認。

             3.11 Contract assets and liabilities                                                  3.11 合約資產及負債

                    Group’s unconditional right to consideration, i.e. only the passage               間流逝即須到期支付代價。本集團確認合約資產
                    of time is required before payment of that consideration is due. The              之減值虧損乃按與貿易應收款項的相同基準。
                    Group recognised impairment loss on contract assets on the same
                    basis as trade receivables.


                    A contract liability represents the Group’s obligation to transfer                合約負債指本集團因已向客戶收取代價（或到期

                    consideration (or an amount of consideration is due) from the

     3.13 Impairment of non-financial assets                                             3.13 非財務資產減值

         Property, plant and equipment, right-of-use assets, intangible assets             物業、廠房及設備、使用權資產、無形資產及於
         and investment in subsidiaries are subject to impairment testing. They            附屬公司之投資須進行減值測試。只要有跡象顯
         are tested for impairment whenever there are indications that the                 示可能不能收回資產賬面值時進行減值測試。
         asset’s carrying amount may not be recoverable.


         An impairment loss is recognised as an expense immediately for the                當資產賬面值高於其可收回金額時，有關金額將
         amount by which the asset’s carrying amount exceeds its recoverable               為減值虧損並會立即確認為開支。可收回金額為
         amount. Recoverable amount is the higher of FVLCOD, and VIU. In                   公平值減出銷售成本與使用價值中之較高者。評
         assessing VIU, the estimated future cash flows are discounted to their            估使用價值時，估計未來現金流量乃以反映金錢
         present value using a pre-tax discount rate that reflects current market          時間值及資產特定風險之現行市場評估之稅前折
         assessment of time value of money and the risk specific to the asset.             現率貼現至其現值。


         For the purposes of assessing impairment, where an asset does not                 為評估減值，倘資產未能帶來大致上獨立於其他
         generate cash inflows largely independent from those from other                   資產之現金流入，則會就可獨立產生現金流入之
         assets, the recoverable amount is determined for the smallest group               最小組別資產（即現金產生單位）釐定可收回金
         of assets that generate cash inflows independently (i.e. a CGU). As a             額。因此，部分資產個別進行減值測試，部分則
         result, some assets are tested individually for impairment and some               在現金產生單位層面進行測試。
         are tested at CGU level.


         Impairment losses recognised for CGUs are charged pro rata to the                 除資產賬面值將不會調減至低於其個別公平值減
         assets in the CGU, except that the carrying value of an asset will not            出售成本或使用價值（如可釐定）外，減值虧損乃
         be reduced below its individual FVLCOD, or VIU, if determinable.                  按比例自該現金產生單位之資產中扣除。


         An impairment loss is reversed if there has been a favourable change              倘用於釐定資產可收回金額之估計出現有利改
         in the estimates used to determine the asset’s recoverable amount                 變，則撥回減值虧損，惟以資產之賬面值不得超
         and only to the extent that the asset’s carrying amount does not                  出於並無確認減值虧損之情況下原應釐定之賬面
         exceed the carrying amount that would have been determined if no                  值（扣除折舊）。
         impairment loss had been recognised.

             3.14 Accounting for income taxes                                                         3.14 所得稅之會計方法

                    Income tax comprises current tax and deferred tax.                                   所得稅包括本期稅項及遞延稅項。


                    Current income tax liabilities comprise those obligations to fiscal                  本期所得稅負債包括於報告日尚未繳付並與本期
                    authorities relating to the current or prior reporting period, that are              或先前申報期有關應向財政當局履行之稅務責
                    unpaid at the reporting date. They are calculated according to the                   任。該等稅務責任乃根據其相關財政期間之適用
                    tax rates and tax laws applicable to the fiscal periods to which they                稅率及稅法，按年度應課稅溢利計算。即期稅項
                    relate, based on the taxable profit for the year. All changes to current             負債的所有變動於損益內確認為所得稅開支的組
                    tax liabilities are recognised as a component of income tax expense in               成部分。
                    profit or loss.


                    Deferred tax is calculated using the liability method on temporary                   遞延稅項乃採用負債法就財務報表之資產及負債
                    differences at the reporting date between the carrying amounts of                    賬面值與彼等各自之課稅基準於報告日之暫時差
                    assets and liabilities in the consolidated financial statements and their            額計算。遞延稅項負債一般就所有應課稅暫時差
                    respective tax bases. Deferred tax liabilities are generally recognised              額確認。
                    for all taxable temporary differences.


                    Deferred tax liabilities are not recognised if the temporary difference              倘暫時差額來自商譽或就對稅務溢利及會計盈虧
                    arises from goodwill or from initial recognition (other than in a business           並無影響之交易初步確認資產及負債（業務合併
                    combination) of assets and liabilities in a transaction that affects                 除外），且不會產生相等的應課稅及可抵扣暫時
                    neither taxable nor accounting profit or loss and does not give rise to              差額，則不會確認遞延稅項負債。
                    equal taxable and deductible temporary differences.


                    Deferred tax is calculated, without discounting, at tax rates that are               遞延稅項不予貼現，並按預期應用於償還負債或
                    expected to apply in the period the liability is settled or the asset is             變現資產期間之稅率計算，惟稅率須於報告日為
                    realised, provided they are enacted or substantively enacted at the                  已頒佈或實際上已頒佈。
                    reporting date.


                    Changes in deferred tax liabilities are recognised in profit or loss.                遞延稅項負債變動會於損益確認。


             3.15 Foreign currency translation                                                        3.15 外幣換算

                    In preparing the financial statements of each individual group entity,               在編製各個別集團實體的財務報表時，以該實體
                    transactions in foreign currencies are recognised at the rates of                    的功能貨幣以外的貨幣（外幣）計價的交易按交易
                    exchanges prevailing on the dates of the transactions. At the end                    日期的適用匯率確認。於報告期末，以外幣為單
                    of the reporting period, monetary items denominated in foreign                       位的貨幣項目均按該日的適用匯率再換算。以歷
                    currencies are retranslated at the rates prevailing at that date. Non-               史成本計量並以外幣計價的非貨幣項目不會再換
                    monetary items that are measured in terms of historical cost in a                    算。
                    foreign currency are not retranslated.


                    Exchange differences arising on the settlement of monetary items, and                因結算貨幣項目及重新換算貨幣項目而產生的匯
                    on the retranslation of monetary items are recognised in profit or loss              兌差額於產生期間在損益中確認。
                    in the period in which they arise.

             The Board considers that the fair values of trade and other receivables which      董事會認為，由於預期將於一年內收回之貿易及其他應
             are expected to be recovered within one year are not materially different from     收款項於開始時之到期期間較短，故該等結餘之公平值

30. EMPLOYEE BENEFIT OBLIGATIONS                                                           30. 僱員福利義務

   Long service payments                                                                     長期服務金

   Under the Employment Ordinance, an employee who has been employed                         根據僱傭條例，按連續性合約受僱且服務年期不少於5
   under a continuous contract for not less than 5 years of service is eligible for          年的僱員，於下列情況下，有權領取長期服務金：
   LSP under the following circumstances:


   •        The employee resigns at age of 65 or above;                                      •   僱員於65歲或以上離職；


   •        The employee dies during employment;                                             •   僱員於受僱期間死亡；


   •        The employee resigns on ground of ill health;                                    •   僱員因健康因素離職；


   •        The fixed term employment contract expires without being renewed;                •   固定期限僱傭合約屆滿且並未重續；


   •        The employee is dismissed which is not because of redundancy or                  •   僱員被解僱並非因裁員或嚴重不當行為
            serious misconduct

36. NOTES TO THE CONSOLIDATED STATEMENT                                                    36. 綜合現金流量表附註

    VALUE MEASUREMENTS

   The Group is exposed to financial risks through its use of financial                    本集團於其日常業務過程中及於其投資活動中使用金融
   instruments in its ordinary course of operations and in its investment                  工具時面臨財務風險。財務風險包括市場風險（包括貨
   activities. The financial risks include market risk (including currency risk,           幣風險、利率風險及其他價格風險）、信貸風險及流動
   interest rate risk and other price risk), credit risk and liquidity risk.               資金風險。


   Financial risk management is coordinated at the Group’s headquarters,                   財務風險管理由本集團總部負責，並定期與董事會緊密
   in close co-operation with the Board periodically. The overall objectives in            合作。管理財務風險的整體目標為透過降低其於金融市
   managing financial risks focus on securing the Group’s short-to-medium-                 場面臨的風險以保持禹銘的中短期現金流量。本集團在
   term cash flows by minimising its exposure to financial markets. Long-                  可接受之風險水平之情況下，管理長期金融投資以產生
   term financial investments are managed to generate lasting returns with                 持久回報。
   acceptable risk levels.

             39.5 Credit risk and impairment assessment                                           39.5 信貸風險及減值評估

                    Credit risk refers to the risk that the counterparty to a financial             信貸風險指金融工具之對手方未能按金融工具之
                    instrument would fail to discharge its obligation under the terms of the        條款履行其責任，並導致本集團錄得財務虧損之
                    financial instrument and cause a financial loss to the Group.                   風險。


                    The credit risk for liquid funds is considered negligible as the                由於流動資金交易對手方乃信譽良好、質素高，
                    counterparties are reputable financial institutions with high quality and       且具備高信貸評級的金融機構，故相關信貸風險
                    credit ratings.                                                                 甚低。


                    The Group adopts conservative investment strategies. Usually                    本集團採納保守投資策略。通常投資為認可證券
                    investments are in liquid securities quoted on recognised stock                 交易所所報之流動證券，惟訂立作長期策略性投
                    exchanges, except where entered into for long-term strategic                    資除外。
                    purposes.


                    For the Group’s investments in listed debt securities and listed                對於本集團的上市債務證券及上市永續證券投
                    perpetual securities, the investment team of the Group assess the               資，本集團的投資團隊會評估發行人的財務表
                    financial performance of the issuers to ensure that the issuers can             現，以確保發行人可償還到期的本金及利息。本
                    satisfy the repayment of the principal and interest as they fall due. The       集團已設定投資組合規模，以控制本集團的信貸
                    Group has set portfolio size limits to control the Group’s exposure to          風險。本集團亦監察各上市債務證券及上市永續
                    the credit risk. The Group also monitors the credit rating and market           證券發行人的信貸評級及市場消息。
                    news of the issuers of respective listed debt securities and listed
                    perpetual securities.


                    The Group is exposed to credit risk on debt securities and perpetual            本集團面臨按公平值計入損益計量之債務證券及
                    securities measured at FVPL, which are not subject to HKFRS 9’s                 永續證券之信貸風險，由於其按公平值計入損益
                    impairment requirements as they are measured at FVPL. The carrying              計量，故不受香港財務報告準則第9號減值規定
                    value of these assets represents the Group’s maximum exposure                   之規限。該等資產之賬面值為於二零二五年十二
                    to credit risk on financial instruments not subject to the HKFRS                月三十一日，本集團就不受香港財務報告準則第
                    9 impairment requirements as at 31 December 2025. Hence, no                     9號減值規定規限之金融工具所面臨之最高信貸
                    separate maximum exposure to credit risk disclosure is provided for             風險。因此，不就該等工具之最高信貸風險單獨
                    these instruments (2024: Nil).                                                  作出披露（二零二四年：零）。


                    The Group’s management considers that all the financial assets                  本集團管理層認為，所有該等財務資產於各報告
                    that are not impaired for each of the reporting dates under review              日期無減值者，均具良好信貸質素。本集團透過
                    are of good credit quality. The Group assessed the credit quality               計及對手方之財務狀況、信貸記錄及其他因素評
                    of the counterparties by taking into account their financial position,          估其信貸質素。管理層亦會定期檢討該等應收款
                    credit history and other factors. Management also regularly reviews             項之可收回性及跟進糾紛或逾期款項（如有）。董
                    the recoverability of these receivables and follow up the disputes or           事認為對手方違約之風險較低。
                    amounts overdue, if any. The Directors are of the opinion that the risk
                    of default by counterparties is low.


                    Generally, the Group considers that the credit risks associated with            整體而言，本集團認為有關按揭貸款及有期貸
                    mortgage loans and term loans are mitigated because they are                    款的信貸風險已減輕，乃由於該等貸款由物業
                    secured over properties and/or guarantee.                                       及╱或擔保作抵押。

   39.6 Liquidity risk                                                                           39.6 流動資金風險

        Liquidity risk relates to the risk that the Group will not be able to meet                    流動資金風險涉及與本集團未能履行以交付現金
        its obligations associated with its financial liabilities that are settled                    或其他財務資產結算財務負債相關責任之風險有
        by delivering cash or other financial assets. The Group is exposed to                         關。本集團在清償其他應付款項及履行融資承擔
        liquidity risk in respect of settlement of other payables and its financing                   方面以及亦在現金流量管理方面承擔流動資金風
        obligations, and also in respect of its cash flow management. The                             險。本集團之目標為維持適當之流動資產水平，
        Group’s objective is to maintain an appropriate level of liquid assets to                     以應付其長短期流動資金需要。
        meet its liquidity requirements in the short and long term.


        The Group manages its liquidity needs by carefully monitoring                                 本集團主要透過謹慎控制長期財務負債的還款期
        scheduled debt servicing payments for long-term financial liabilities as                      及日常業務現金流出，以管理其流動資金需要。
        well as cash-outflows due in day-to-day business. Liquidity needs are                         本集團按日監察其流動資金需求，另每月釐定為
        monitored on a day-to-day basis. Long-term liquidity needs for 360-                           期360日的長期流動資金需要。
        day lookout period are identified monthly.


        The Group maintains cash, short-term bank deposits and marketable                             本集團維持現金、短期銀行存款及有價證券以應
        securities to meet its liquidity requirements for up to 30-day periods.                       付未來最多30日期間之流動資金需要。透過出
        Funding for long-term liquidity needs is secured by the ability to sell                       售長期財務資產之能力保障長期流動資金需要。
        long-term financial assets.

        non-derivative financial liabilities as at 31 December 2025 and 2024.                         十二月三十一日之非衍生財務負債之剩餘合約期

             39.7 Fair value measurements                                                                 39.7 公平值計量

                    Financial assets carried at fair value                                                  按公平值列賬之財務資產

                    The following table presents financial assets measured at fair value                     下表呈列根據公平值層級，於報告日按公平值計
                    at the reporting date in accordance with fair value hierarchy. The                       量的財務資產之資料。此層級根據計量此等財務
                    hierarchy groups financial assets into three levels based on the relative                資產及負債之公平值所使用的重要輸入數據的相
                    reliability of significant inputs used in measuring the fair value of these              對可靠性，將財務資產劃分為三層。公平值層級
                    financial assets and liabilities. The fair value hierarchy has the following             分為以下各層：
                    levels:


                    –         Level 1: quoted prices (unadjusted) in active markets for                      －     第一層：相同資產及負債於活躍市場的報
                              identical assets and liabilities;                                                    價（未作調整）；


                    –         Level 2: inputs other than quoted prices included within Level                 －     第二層：第一層所包括之報價以外就該資
                              1 that are observable for the asset or liability, either directly (i.e.              產或負債可觀察之輸入數據，可為直接
                              as prices) or indirectly (i.e. derived from prices); and                            （即如價格）或間接（即源自價格）；及


                    –         Level 3: inputs for the asset or liability that are not based on               －     第三層：資產或負債並非依據可觀察市場
                              observable market data (unobservable inputs).                                        數據之輸入數據（非可觀察輸入數據）。

40. CAPITAL MANAGEMENT                                                              40. 資本管理

   The Group’s capital management objectives are to ensure the Group’s ability        本集團之資本管理目標為確保本集團有能力以持續經營
   to continue as a going concern and to provide an adequate return to the            基準運作及為股東提供足夠回報。
   shareholders.

   adjustments to the capital structure in light of changes in economic               調整資本結構。本集團以負債與權益比率為基準監控其
   conditions. The Group monitors its capital structure on the basis of debt to       資本結構。

41. LITIGATIONS (Continued)                                                           41. 訴訟（續）

   The Group’s legal advisor is of the view that Yu Ming is entitled to rely on the     本集團法律顧問認為，禹銘有權依據「內部管理規則」，
   “indoor management rule”, which states that any person contracting with              當中規定任何人士與一間公司訂立合約及與該公司以誠
   a company and dealing in good faith with the same may assume that acts               信進行交易時，可假設其規章及權力已妥善及正式履行
   within its constitution and powers have been properly and duly performed             的事實，且不受內部管理行為是否定期進行的約束。彼
   and are not bound to inquire whether acts of internal management have                等亦認為，同期記錄及通信證明禹銘根據委聘函對原告
   been regular. They are also of the view that there are contemporaneous               所作的工作。根據禹銘向其提供的資料及文件，本集團
   records and correspondence evidencing Yu Ming’s work done for the                    法律顧問認為，撇除訴訟的無常變化禹銘有頗大機會就
   plaintiff pursuant to the engagement letters. Based on the information               該訴訟成功抗辯。
   and documents provided to it by Yu Ming, the Group’s legal advisor is of
   the opinion that, the vagaries of litigation aside, Yu Ming is very likely to
   successfully defend the Action.


   The Directors, having obtained the legal opinion from the legal advisor,             董事已獲得法律顧問的法律意見，認為因上述訴訟而產
   considered that the financial impact arising from providing the above litigation     生的財務影響輕微，因此截至二零二五年及二零二四年
   is insignificant and accordingly, no provision in respect of this case was made      十二月三十一日止年度內毋須就本案件計提撥備。
   for the years ended 31 December 2025 and 2024.
